[[sec-090-smc_efuse_jtag_lc_access_matrix_test]]
===== 90 `smc_efuse_jtag_lc_access_matrix_test`

*Source Module*: `smc_efuse_jtag_lc_access_matrix_test`

*BFM / Model*: SPI pad BFM, ocah_axi_vip (AXI), ocah_axi_vip (AXI-Lite), ocah_jtag_vip

*Objective*: Drive lc_state_i + JTAG eFuse accesses; assert the block/allow matrix.

*Test Coverage*:

* `tb_lc_state` drives `lc_state_i` = {diff_n, diff_p} directly, and
* the `ej_axi` AXI-Lite master drives `axil_smc_otp_jtag_req_i`.
* The expected matrix is taken from the specification, not the RTL: `hw/ip/efuse/doc/architecture.adoc`, section "Lifecycle State (LC_STATE) Effects", cited below by line.
* lifecycle encodings, :265-282 -- `TEST_DEV 0x0 | PROD 0x1 | RMA_SOP 0x2, 0x3 | RMA_CHIPLET 0x6-0x7 | PROD_END 0x8 | INVALID all others`;
* the restricted set, :286 -- "When lifecycle state is PROD (0x1) or RMA_SOP (0x2, 0x3), JTAG access to eFuse registers is restricted";
* the access table, :289-295 -- JTAG Write *Blocked* / JTAG Read *Blocked* except Chiplet ID / JTAG Read Chiplet ID Allowed, in both columns;
* the Chiplet ID exception, :301-305 -- "Even in PROD or RMA_SOP states, JTAG read access to the chiplet ID and package ID registers is permitted";
* the blocked response, :297-299 -- "the error slave returns an error response with data value `0xbadcab1e`".

The matrix below is the specification's outcome per raw `lc_state_i`
encoding; the encodings and state names are the specification's
(`hw/ip/efuse/doc/architecture.adoc:265-282`). The checkers drive one
encoding per state -- `0x0`, `0x1`, `0x2`, `0x6`, `0x8` -- plus the
integrity-error case; `0x3` and `0x7`, the specification's second encodings
of RMA_SOP and RMA_CHIPLET, are not driven. The integrity-error row is not a
specification table row: it is the `INVALID ... (error fallback)` class
(`:281`) applied fail-closed, with no identity exception.
`smc_efuse_wrapper` gates JTAG-to-eFuse access on `lc_state_i`; a blocked
transaction returns DECERR with data `0xBADCAB1E`. `smc_efuse_jtag_lc_negative_test`
covers the CSR-observable subset (`CHIP_CONFIG_LC_STATE` plus the always-allowed
JTAG_PUBLIC_IDENTITY reads).

[cols="2,2,2,3",options="header",]
|===
|SMC LC state (raw) |JTAG eFuse write |JTAG eFuse read (non-ID) |JTAG read JTAG_PUBLIC_IDENTITY
|TEST_DEV `0x0` |allow |allow |allow
|PROD `0x1` |BLOCK (DECERR/`0xBADCAB1E`) |BLOCK |allow
|RMA_SOP `0x2`,`0x3` |BLOCK |BLOCK |allow
|RMA_CHIPLET `0x6`,`0x7` |allow |allow |allow
|PROD_END `0x8` |allow |allow |allow
|differential-decode integrity error |BLOCK |BLOCK |BLOCK (no ID exception)
|===

*Test Procedure*:

Drive `smc_efuse_jtag_lc_access_matrix_test_seq`. Each checker below is a named compare the DUT can fail; a token is emitted only after that compare holds.


*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-EFUSE-JTAG-LC-PROD` |Named compare in `smc_efuse_jtag_lc_access_matrix_test.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-EFUSE-JTAG-LC-PROD_END` |Named compare in `smc_efuse_jtag_lc_access_matrix_test.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-EFUSE-JTAG-LC-RMA_CHIPLET` |Named compare in `smc_efuse_jtag_lc_access_matrix_test.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-EFUSE-JTAG-LC-RMA_SOP` |Named compare in `smc_efuse_jtag_lc_access_matrix_test.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-EFUSE-JTAG-LC-SIGINT` |Named compare in `smc_efuse_jtag_lc_access_matrix_test.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-EFUSE-JTAG-LC-TEST_DEV` |Named compare in `smc_efuse_jtag_lc_access_matrix_test.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|===


*Pass Criteria*: `CHK-EFUSE-JTAG-LC-PROD`, `CHK-EFUSE-JTAG-LC-PROD_END`, `CHK-EFUSE-JTAG-LC-RMA_CHIPLET`, `CHK-EFUSE-JTAG-LC-RMA_SOP`, `CHK-EFUSE-JTAG-LC-SIGINT`, `CHK-EFUSE-JTAG-LC-TEST_DEV` hold after their compares. Enrolled in: `all`, `hosted`.
'''''

[[sec-097-smc_efuse_permission_boundary_test]]
===== 97 `smc_efuse_permission_boundary_test`

*Source Module*: `smc_efuse_permission_boundary_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip (AXI)

*Objective*: Read the four eFuse-derived chip-config registers over SEP_IN AXI on the CSR side of the eFuse boundary, with the eFuse-bank AXI-Lite positive control and idle leg.

*Test Coverage*:

* DEFENDS: the four eFuse-derived chip-config registers decode; `VERSION_LO`, `VERSION_HI` and `CHIP_ID` read their generated reset values, `LC_STATE` is decode-only because its value is simulator-divergent in the OSS bench; and the eFuse-bank AXI-Lite probe is observed going 0 -> 1 -> 0 (positive control then idle leg).
* DOES NOT DEFEND: eFuse permission semantics. This testcase runs the same `smc_efuse_chip_config_read_test_seq` body as testcase 86 -- it programs no lock, drives no lifecycle transition and attempts no denied access, because the SMC CSR boundary exposes none of that. Lifecycle-gated access control is proven by `smc_efuse_jtag_lc_access_matrix_test` and locked-shadow access by `smc_efuse_locked_access_interrupt_test`; the raw OTP window has no responder on `smc_wrapper` and is recorded under Known Limitations.
* PROXY: the protocol-VIP record this leaf files carries `proxy=True` and names what it stands in for -- the raw OTP permission paths -- and what it did not do (no lock programmed, no lifecycle transition, no denied access, no raw OTP window touched). The record is not feature credit for that path.

*Test Procedure*:

Drive `smc_efuse_chip_config_read_test_seq`. Each checker below is a named compare the DUT can fail; a token is emitted only after that compare holds.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]

|`CHK-EFUSE-BANK-IDLE` |Named compare in `smc_efuse_vip_utils.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|===


*Pass Criteria*: `CHK-EFUSE-BANK-AXIL-ACTIVE`, `CHK-EFUSE-BANK-IDLE` hold after their compares. Enrolled in: `all`, `hosted`.
'''''

[[sec-098-smc_efuse_read_program_timeout_test]]
===== 98 `smc_efuse_read_program_timeout_test`

*Source Module*: `smc_efuse_read_program_timeout_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip (AXI)

*Objective*: timeout_enable|0 aborts; default timeout recovers OTP bit0.

*Test Coverage*:

* PROGRAM/READ timeout CSRs.
* timeout_enable|0 fires in ST_WAIT_RESP on this shim.
* **The claim is split, and only one half is a DUT-path claim.** *DUT path (reportable).* `EFUSE_PROGRAM_REQ_TIMEOUT` / `EFUSE_READ_REQ_TIMEOUT` with `timeout_enable=1, cycles=0` must abort the outstanding request and raise `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL.PROGRAM_STATUS` / leave `EFUSE_READ_INTERFACE_READ_DATA` empty; re-programming the RDL-default cycle count must let the same operation complete.
* That FSM is real `efuse_interface_controller` RTL and every address, field mask and reset used here is imported by generated symbol from `efuse_interface_ctrl.h`.
* *Model-backed (NOT silicon-path coverage).* `tb_efuse_programmed_word0` is a TB tap on `u_dut.u_smc_ip_integration.u_efuse_bank_model.u_efuse_bank_reg` (`tb/tb_top.sv:1274-1277`).
* SPEC declares that block a stand-in: "The eFuse bank model (`efuse_bank_model.sv`) is a reference, simulation-only stand-in for the real foundry OTP macro ...
* In a production integration it is replaced by the actual foundry macro driven by the SHIM" (`hw/ip/efuse/doc/architecture.adoc:163-170`), and its set-once semantics are `onwrite = woset` in the DV RDL `hw/ip/efuse/dv/models/regs/efuse_bank.rdl:14`.
* The `OTP=...` observations below therefore show that the controller's command did or did not reach the bank model -- they are **not** proof that a fuse burns in silicon ([BEHAVIORAL-STUB-DECLARED], §2 real-path/no-HW-equivalent).

*Test Procedure*:

Drive `smc_efuse_read_program_timeout_test_seq`. Each checker below is a named compare the DUT can fail; a token is emitted only after that compare holds.


*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-EFUSE-READ-STATUS-SET-ON-NO-ENABLE` |Positive control for the `READ_STATUS == 0` compares: with `STATUS.EFUSE_REQ_ERROR` read clear first, the recovery read's command word minus `READ_ENABLE` sets `READ_STATUS` in `EFUSE_READ_CTRL`, the cause `efuse_interface_ctrl.rdl` names for the field; `READ_DATA` is logged, not asserted (`smc_efuse_read_program_timeout_test_seq.py`)
|`CHK-EFUSE-TMO-BASIC` |Named compare in `smc_efuse_read_program_timeout_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-EFUSE-TMO-PROG` |Named compare in `smc_efuse_read_program_timeout_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-EFUSE-TMO-PROG-REC` |Named compare in `smc_efuse_read_program_timeout_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-EFUSE-TMO-PROG-SET` |Named compare in `smc_efuse_read_program_timeout_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-EFUSE-TMO-RD` |Named compare in `smc_efuse_read_program_timeout_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-EFUSE-TMO-RD-REC` |Named compare in `smc_efuse_read_program_timeout_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|===


*Pass Criteria*: `CHK-EFUSE-READ-STATUS-SET-ON-NO-ENABLE`, `CHK-EFUSE-TMO-BASIC`, `CHK-EFUSE-TMO-PROG`, `CHK-EFUSE-TMO-PROG-REC`, `CHK-EFUSE-TMO-PROG-SET`, `CHK-EFUSE-TMO-RD` (and 1 more) hold after their compares. Enrolled in: `all`, `hosted`.
'''''

[[sec-099-smc_efuse_read_timeout_status_test]]
===== 99 `smc_efuse_read_timeout_status_test`

*Source Module*: `smc_efuse_read_timeout_status_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip (AXI) against the eFuse bank model

*Objective*: A read that hits `EFUSE_READ_REQ_TIMEOUT` completes with `EFUSE_READ_CTRL.READ_STATUS` set, after a successful read shows it clear.

*Test Coverage*:

* `hw/ip/efuse/regs/efuse_interface_ctrl.rdl` defines `EFUSE_READ_CTRL.READ_STATUS` as the read interface's error report (`read_done` says the read completed, `read_status` says how) and `EFUSE_READ_REQ_TIMEOUT` as a bound on the cycles the interface waits for a SHIM response; `hw/ip/efuse/doc/programming.adoc` has software check `read_status` after every completion. A read that hits the bound is a completed read with an error: `read_done=1` and `read_status=1`.
* The arming leg makes the check real: `READ_STATUS` holds until the next completion, so a stale 1 would satisfy the timeout leg on its own. A successful read at the RDL-default cycle count comes first and must show `READ_STATUS` 0; `STATUS.EFUSE_REQ_ERROR` is required clear beforehand because a sticky request error also reports through `READ_STATUS`.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Issue a successful read at the RDL default READ_TMO and record READ_CTRL with READ_STATUS clear.
. Require STATUS.EFUSE_REQ_ERROR clear, so a sticky req-err cannot be mistaken for the timeout path.
. Force the read to time out and read READ_CTRL again.
. Require READ_STATUS to be set.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-EFUSE-TMO-RD-STATUS-ARM` |A successful read at the RDL-default timeout completes with `READ_DONE` set and `READ_STATUS` clear, with `STATUS.EFUSE_REQ_ERROR` clear, arming the leg below (`smc_efuse_read_timeout_status_test_seq.py`)
|`CHK-EFUSE-TMO-RD-STATUS-SET` |The read that hits the programmed timeout completes with `READ_DONE` set and `READ_STATUS` set (`smc_efuse_read_timeout_status_test_seq.py`)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: `CHK-EFUSE-TMO-RD-STATUS-ARM`, `CHK-EFUSE-TMO-RD-STATUS-SET` hold after their compares. Enrolled in: `all`, `hosted`.

'''''

[[sec-101-smc_state_fault_inject_test]]
===== 101 `smc_state_fault_inject_test`

*Source Module*: `smc_state_fault_inject_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip (AXI) for the CSR path; state corruption is a force
on the state flops through the testbench's `tb_efuse_program_state_inject`,
`tb_efuse_read_state_inject` and `tb_zeroer_state_inject` hooks (each with its
`_en`); the read errors are forces through `tb_efuse_read_error_inject` on the
eFuse interface response status, `efuse_req_err_i` and `secure_tm_blocked_i`;
and the SYS_OUT responder's observability counters (`tb_output_axi_write_count`,
`tb_output_axi_last_addr`, `tb_output_axi_last_wdata`) for the Zeroer's fabric
traffic

*Objective*: Every unused state encoding in the eFuse interface controller and in the Zeroer fails closed, and the block recovers.

*Test Coverage*:

* Directed fault injection into two state machines: the eFuse interface
  controller's program and read FSMs, and the Zeroer FSM.
* eFuse: both unused 2-bit encodings (`0b00` and `0b11`) are injected, on the
  program path and the read path independently.
* The same-cycle claim is the point of the `0b11` case -- the corruption is armed
  concurrently with a legal `GO` write, and the injector asserts the request line
  is suppressed in that same cycle rather than one cycle later.
* Recovery is scored, not just entry: after injection the FSM must return to
  `IDLE` with request low, busy low, done high and error high, and the program
  read-back must read the `0xBADCAB1E` illegal-state sentinel.
* Non-vacuity is a real baseline: a legal program runs first and the sequence
  asserts its read-back does *not* already equal the sentinel, so the
  post-injection compare could have failed.
* Read-error sourcing: a new legal request must clear a prior read error, and an
  injected read error must set the status and zero the read-back.
* Zeroer: all four illegal encodings (`0b011`, `0b101`, `0b110`, `0b111`) must
  enter the absorbing `ERROR` state, hold it for at least two further cycles with
  busy high, `intp` low and both AXI valids low, and only a cold reset escapes it.
* Each of the four "start write is ignored in `ERROR`" legs has its own live
  control, run immediately before its injection: the pass-all inbound/outbound
  output filters are programmed, then the same `CTRL_STATUS` write (`INT_EN`,
  packed from the generated `ZEROER_CTRL_CTRL_STATUS_reg_t` layout), with
  `DEST_ADDR`/`SIZE` programmed to one 8-byte beat at the output fabric, starts
  the Zeroer from `IDLE`: busy is sampled high, the FSM is seen somewhere other
  than `IDLE` during that busy interval and never in `ERROR`, the SYS_OUT
  responder counts exactly one write beat of zero at that address, and the FSM
  returns to `IDLE` with `CTRL_STATUS` busy clear.
* The control is per encoding because the cold reset that ends each encoding is
  also the output filters' reset (`smc_internal_regs.sv`:
  `filter_ctrl_reg.arst_n` is `rst_primary_smc_clk_ni`), and a read-back after
  that reset returns the reset word rather than pass-all. Programming the
  filters once would leave the deny's "the responder write count did not move"
  able to hold because the SYS_OUT write path was shut, not because the Zeroer
  refused the start. With the control immediately before the injection, the
  identical write is measured moving that counter under the same filter
  configuration the deny runs under, and nothing resets the filters between the
  two -- so the deny differs from its control in the FSM state alone.
* Every wait is bounded (10 000 cycles on the FSM and CSR waits, 200 cycles on
  the output-responder observation) and reports the last observed value on
  expiry, so a signal that never moves fails rather than hanging.

*Test Procedure*:

The DUT offers no frontdoor way to reach an unused state encoding, so each
encoding is forced through the `tb_*_state_inject` hook named above and each
read error through `tb_efuse_read_error_inject`. The checks read the state flop
back through the `tb_efuse_program_state` / `tb_efuse_read_state` /
`tb_zeroer_state` taps and the blocks' internal outputs (`tb_efuse_*_busy`,
`tb_efuse_*_done`, `tb_efuse_*_error`, `tb_efuse_*_readback`, `tb_zeroer_*`),
not the control registers; that is why the leaf is held out of `all` and
recorded under Known Limitations.

[arabic]
. Wait for fuse-sense-done.
. Run a legal program command, confirm completion, and record a read-back that is
  not the illegal-state sentinel.
. Inject eFuse program state `0b00` with no request outstanding; confirm the
  request stays low in the corrupted cycle, recovery reaches `IDLE` with done and
  error set, `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` reads done with status set and busy clear, and
  the read-back is the sentinel. Run a legal program and confirm it completes
  without error and its read-back is no longer the sentinel.
. Arm the `0b11` injection concurrently with a legal program `GO`, confirm the
  request is suppressed in the same cycle, then confirm the same recovery and the
  same legal program afterwards.
. Repeat both encodings on the read path, each followed by a legal read that
  completes without error; inject a read error and confirm the status sets and
  the read-back zeroes, then clear it through `REQ_ERROR_CLEAR`.
. For each of the four illegal Zeroer encodings, run its start control first:
  program the inbound/outbound output-fabric filters to pass all, confirm the
  Zeroer is in `IDLE` with `CTRL_STATUS` not reporting busy, program `DEST_ADDR`
  to the output-fabric address and `SIZE` to 8, then write `CTRL_STATUS` with
  `INT_EN` while sampling busy every cycle; confirm busy rose, the FSM was seen
  somewhere other than `IDLE` during the busy interval and never in `ERROR`, the
  SYS_OUT responder saw exactly one write of zero at that address, and the FSM is
  back in `IDLE` with `CTRL_STATUS` busy clear.
. Then, for the same encoding: start from `IDLE` with `CTRL_STATUS` not reporting
  busy, re-arm the same `DEST_ADDR`/`SIZE`, inject, confirm the absorbing `ERROR`
  state holds with `CTRL_STATUS` reporting busy, write `CTRL_STATUS` as the
  control just did and confirm the start is ignored for 200 cycles with the
  responder write count unchanged, then assert cold reset and confirm the escape
  to `IDLE` with `CTRL_STATUS` clear. The cold reset also clears the filters, so
  the next encoding's control re-programs them.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-ZEROER-START-CONTROL` |Emitted once per illegal Zeroer encoding, immediately before that encoding's injection: with the pass-all output filters programmed and the Zeroer in `IDLE` with `DEST_ADDR`/`SIZE` set, the `CTRL_STATUS` start write raises busy with the FSM seen outside `IDLE` during the busy interval and never in `ERROR`, the SYS_OUT responder counts exactly one write beat of zero at `DEST_ADDR`, and the FSM returns to `IDLE` with `CTRL_STATUS` busy clear -- so the SYS_OUT write path is measured open under the filter configuration the deny that follows runs under
|`CHK-STATE-FAULT` |eFuse `00`/`11` fail closed on both the program and the read path, with done and status set in `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` / `EFUSE_READ_CTRL`, and a legal operation completes without error afterwards; a new legal request clears a prior read error and an injected read error sets the status; Zeroer `011`/`101`/`110`/`111` each enter the absorbing error state from `IDLE`, report busy in `CTRL_STATUS`, ignore the `CTRL_STATUS` start write that their own control had just run from `IDLE` over a path shown open (state, busy, `intp`, AXI valids and the responder write count all unchanged), and only a cold reset escapes it
|===


*Pass Criteria*: `CHK-ZEROER-START-CONTROL` holds for all four illegal Zeroer encodings and `CHK-STATE-FAULT` holds, both after their compares; each deny's unchanged responder write count is preceded by that encoding's own control moving the same counter; and every bounded wait in the sequence completed rather than expiring. Enrolled in: `all`, `hosted`.

'''''

[[sec-093-smc_efuse_map_read_test]]
===== 93 `smc_efuse_map_read_test`

*Source Module*: `smc_efuse_map_read_test`

*BFM / Model*: I3C VIP, SPI pad BFM, ocah_axi_vip (AXI)

*Objective*: SMC_EFUSE_MAP 1 KiB window direct read.

*Test Coverage*:

* The sweep reads LOCKS lo/hi, `JTAG_PUBLIC_IDENTITY`, `SMC_CONFIG`, `OCCP_TRANSPORT_TIMEOUT`, all nine `I2C_I3C_ID` entries, and SPARE[0:1] -- every region that has both a generated base address and a generated `*_READ_LOCK` bit, plus LOCKS itself.
* readable words -- `efuse_preload_word_at(addr)` parses `hw/sys/smc/dv/assets/smc_efuse_default.hex` at run time and indexes it by `(addr - SMC_TOP_SMC_EFUSE_MAP_BASE_ADDR)/4`, the same word ordering `efuse_bank_model.sv:140-160` uses. Regenerating the asset moves the expectation with it instead of leaving a stale literal asserting a false identity.
* read-locked words -- `efuse_map_read_locked()` reads the `*_READ_LOCK` bit out of the *preloaded* `LOCKS` word using the generated bit position from `regs/gen/c/blocks/smc_efuse_map.h`; SPEC then fixes the data a blocked read returns: "When a request is blocked, the error slave returns an error response with data value `0xbadcab1e`" (`architecture.adoc:297-299`), and `lock[0] = 1` is "read-locked" (`architecture.adoc:198`).

*Test Procedure*:

Drive `smc_efuse_map_read_test_seq`. Each checker below is a named compare the DUT can fail; a token is emitted only after that compare holds.


*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-EFUSE-MAP-READ` |Named compare in `smc_efuse_map_read_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|===


*Pass Criteria*: `CHK-EFUSE-MAP-READ` hold after their compares. Enrolled in: `all`, `hosted`.
'''''

[[sec-088-smc_efuse_boundary_signals_test]]
===== 88 `smc_efuse_boundary_signals_test`

*Source Module*: `smc_efuse_boundary_signals_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip (AXI)

*Objective*: Sense completes under ext_boot hold; delayed fuse_reset_n waits release.

*Test Coverage*:

* `LOCKS` -- read from `hw/sys/smc/dv/assets/smc_efuse_default.hex` at run time via `efuse_preload_word_at` rather than a hand-transcribed literal, which cannot detect the asset and the model disagreeing. That asset is what `efuse_bank_model.sv:140-160` `$readmemh`s under the bench-wide `+smc_efuse_hex` (`smc_sim_cfg.toml:386` and `:392`), so this leg's proof class is **transport** (the map returns the sensed word), not fuse programming ([NO-UNJUSTIFIED-PRELOAD]).
* `EFUSE_PROGRAM_INTERFACE_READ_DATA` -- the generated RDL reset `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL__EFUSE_PROGRAM_INTERFACE_READ_DATA__DOUT_reset` (`hw/ip/efuse/regs/gen/c/efuse_interface_ctrl.h:153`); no program read-back has been issued in this testcase, so the register must still hold it. That reset is `0x0` and the field is `sw = r`, so this compare is a transport/decode probe and **not** a liveness proof: a dead or unmapped window satisfies it equally. Since the field is driven only in the program FSM's `ST_CAPTURE_DATA` state ("Hardware debug only", `efuse_interface_ctrl.rdl:158`), which this testcase never enters, no distinguishing value can be placed in it. Block liveness is instead proven on the writable sibling `EFUSE_READ_REQ_TIMEOUT` @0x14 (`sw=rw`, `rdl:180`, non-zero reset `0x800000`) by a reset read plus a write/read-back/restore in the same run ([NEGATIVE-NEEDS-POSITIVE-CONTROL]).
* `SCRATCH_COLD_WARM_0` -- its own reset value 0, backed in the same run by a write/read-back positive control proving the register can hold a one ([NEGATIVE-NEEDS-POSITIVE-CONTROL]).

*Test Procedure*:

Drive `smc_efuse_boundary_signals_test_seq`. Each checker below is a named compare the DUT can fail; a token is emitted only after that compare holds.


*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-EFUSE-BND-BASIC` |Named compare in `smc_efuse_boundary_signals_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-EFUSE-BND-HOLD` |Named compare in `smc_efuse_boundary_signals_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-EFUSE-BND-MAP` |Named compare in `smc_efuse_boundary_signals_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-EFUSE-BND-REL` |Named compare in `smc_efuse_boundary_signals_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-EFUSE-BND-STAY` |Named compare in `smc_efuse_boundary_signals_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|===


*Pass Criteria*: `CHK-EFUSE-BND-BASIC`, `CHK-EFUSE-BND-HOLD`, `CHK-EFUSE-BND-MAP`, `CHK-EFUSE-BND-REL`, `CHK-EFUSE-BND-STAY` hold after their compares. Enrolled in: `all`, `hosted`.
'''''

[[sec-092-smc_efuse_locked_access_interrupt_test]]
===== 92 `smc_efuse_locked_access_interrupt_test`

*Source Module*: `smc_efuse_locked_access_interrupt_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip (AXI), ocah_jtag_vip

*Objective*: JTAG_PUBLIC_IDENTITY unlocked write silent; locked write/read pulse `tb_efuse_locked_access_irq`.

*Test Coverage*:

* Locked eFuse shadow access raises `tb_efuse_locked_access_irq`.
* DECLARED PRECONDITION -- the read-lock is supplied by the bench, not the DUT.
* `JTAG_PUBLIC_IDENTITY` is read-locked before this sequence does anything because word 0 of `hw/sys/smc/dv/assets/smc_efuse_default.hex` has `JTAG_PUBLIC_IDENTITY_READ_LOCK` set (LOCKS bit 1), and the adopter-supplied simulation stand-in for the OTP macro, `hw/ip/efuse/dv/models/efuse_bank_model.sv`, `$readmemh`s that asset into the bank at time 0 under `+smc_efuse_hex` (named on this testcase's `[[tests]]` entry in `hw/sys/smc/dv/testlists/holdout.toml`).
* The read-lock leg therefore proves that the DUT ENFORCES a lock it found already set; it does not prove a lock can be established through the fuse-programming path.
* The write-lock leg does establish its own lock, through the real `LOCKS` CSR write path.
* Everything asserted below -- lock enforcement, the non-disclosure property and the interrupt itself -- is produced by the design under test.
* The read-locked read is not value-compared: `hw/ip/efuse/doc/architecture.adoc` fixes `0xbadcab1e` only for the JTAG lifecycle-demux error slave and does not state what a read-locked shadow register returns over the CSR path, so that leg claims the interrupt pulse and non-disclosure (`lock[0] = 1` is read-locked) and nothing about the substituted word.
* The model contributes the fuse CONTENT only, and every expectation that depends on that content is derived from the asset at run time rather than hand-transcribed.

*Test Procedure*:

Drive `smc_efuse_locked_access_interrupt_test_seq`. Each checker below is a named compare the DUT can fail; a token is emitted only after that compare holds.


*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-EFUSE-LOCK-IRQ-RD` |The read of the read-locked `JTAG_PUBLIC_IDENTITY` pulses `tb_efuse_locked_access_irq` exactly once and the word it returns is neither the fuse content nor the pattern the unlocked write attempted (sequence asserts). The word itself is not value-compared: no document fixes what a read-locked shadow register returns over the CSR path (`smc_efuse_locked_access_interrupt_test_seq.py`)
|`CHK-EFUSE-LOCK-IRQ-UNLOCK` |Named compare in `smc_efuse_locked_access_interrupt_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-EFUSE-LOCK-IRQ-WR` |Named compare in `smc_efuse_locked_access_interrupt_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-EFUSE-LOCK-PRE` |Named compare in `smc_efuse_locked_access_interrupt_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-EFUSE-LOCK-SCOREBOARD` |Named compare in `smc_efuse_locked_access_interrupt_test.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|===


*Pass Criteria*: `CHK-EFUSE-LOCK-IRQ-RD`, `CHK-EFUSE-LOCK-IRQ-UNLOCK`, `CHK-EFUSE-LOCK-IRQ-WR`, `CHK-EFUSE-LOCK-PRE`, `CHK-EFUSE-LOCK-SCOREBOARD` hold after their compares. Enrolled in: `all`, `hosted`.
'''''

[[sec-094-smc_efuse_otp_burn_shadow_test]]
===== 94 `smc_efuse_otp_burn_shadow_test`

*Source Module*: `smc_efuse_otp_burn_shadow_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip (AXI)

*Objective*: Fuse sense + shadow read + PROGRAM command/status through DUT CSRs.

*Test Coverage*:

* Real fuse sense completes without `+skip_fuse_sense`, and the SMC_EFUSE_MAP window serves the sensed word over SEP_IN AXI (`efuse_shadow_regs.sv:674-679`, loaded by the sense FSM at `efuse_shadow_regs.sv:512-540`).
* The eFuse interface controller issues a PROGRAM command, latches the readback comparison result and reports it in `PROGRAM_STATUS` (`efuse_interface_shim.sv:433` -> `efuse_program_interface.sv:160`), with both polarities observed: set on the injected failure, clear on the clean program.
* `EFUSE_STATUS.efuse_sense_done` mirrors the sense handshake.
* The bank model's storage is unchanged by a fail-injected program and sticky-ORs one bit on a successful one. This shows the controller's command reached the bank; it says nothing about fuse physics.
* Foundry OTP macro analog timing / voltage.
* Full OCCP ROM secure-boot stack (see smc_occp_sanity_secure_error_test).

*Test Procedure*:

Drive `smc_efuse_otp_burn_shadow_test_seq`. Each checker below is a named compare the DUT can fail; a token is emitted only after that compare holds.


*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-EFUSE-OTP-PRELOAD` |Named compare in `smc_efuse_otp_burn_shadow_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-EFUSE-OTP-PROGRAM-FAIL` |Named compare in `smc_efuse_otp_burn_shadow_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-EFUSE-OTP-PROGRAM-OK` |Named compare in `smc_efuse_otp_burn_shadow_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-EFUSE-OTP-SHADOW` |Named compare in `smc_efuse_otp_burn_shadow_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-EFUSE-OTP-STATUS` |Named compare in `smc_efuse_otp_burn_shadow_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|===


*Pass Criteria*: `CHK-EFUSE-OTP-PRELOAD`, `CHK-EFUSE-OTP-PROGRAM-FAIL`, `CHK-EFUSE-OTP-PROGRAM-OK`, `CHK-EFUSE-OTP-SHADOW`, `CHK-EFUSE-OTP-STATUS` hold after their compares. Enrolled in: `all`, `hosted`.
'''''

[[sec-103-smc_gpio_filter_access_sep_test]]
===== 103 `smc_gpio_filter_access_sep_test`

*Source Module*: `smc_gpio_filter_access_sep_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip (AXI), ocah_axi_vip (AXI-Lite)

*Objective*: GPIO0/1 ACCESS_FILTER: AxPROT=1 allowed, AxPROT=0 refused (read + write).

*Test Coverage*:

* READ half -- an unprivileged read is refused with DECERR, the code the GPIO programming guide specifies for a blocked transaction, and the error-slave signature 0xBADCAB1E, while the privileged read returns the programmed word.
* WRITE half -- an unprivileged write of the value that would DISARM the filter is refused, and a privileged read afterwards shows the register unchanged, so the refusal is proven to have taken no effect. The refusal code is asserted as "an error response" rather than an exact code: the GPIO programming guide (`hw/ip/gpio/doc/programming.adoc`, "Filter Configuration") specifies DECERR for a blocked transaction, which the read half requires exactly, while the write half is observed at the SEP_IN AXI port as SLVERR and no document this bench can cite fixes how the SMC fabric forwards a write-channel refusal.

*Test Procedure*:

Drive `smc_gpio_filter_access_sep_test_seq`. Each checker below is a named compare the DUT can fail; a token is emitted only after that compare holds.

* then takes the three spaced samples. Sample 0 is the reference; samples 1..N-1 carry its `core2pad_en_vec` as `expect_core2pad_en_vec`, and the scoreboard books that exact compare as *checked* evidence because step 1 credited the probe in this same run.

|===


*Pass Criteria*: `CHK-GPIO-P0-MUX-LSIO`, `CHK-GPIO-P0-MUX-PATTERN`, `CHK-GPIO-P0-MUX-PRIO`, `CHK-GPIO-P0-MUX-REG` hold after their compares. Enrolled in: `all`, `hosted`, `gpio`.
'''''

[[sec-186-smc_external_window_pad_ctrl_decode_test]]
===== 186 `smc_external_window_pad_ctrl_decode_test`

*Source Module*: `smc_external_window_pad_ctrl_decode_test`

*BFM / Model*: SEP_IN AXI driver; `tb_axil_external_active`, a mirror of `smc_external_req_o`, sampled on every `clk_smc_i` edge while each access is in flight; nothing in this bench answers behind the window

*Objective*: The six mandatory external-window control blocks and the first, second and last of the 65 per-pad control blocks decode into the adopter external AXI-Lite window.

*Test Coverage*:

* Addresses come from the generated bootrom register header, the same map the boot ROM is built against.
* The adopter's implementation of these blocks is outside this repository, so nothing behind the window is specified or credited here. What the SMC side owns, and what is measured here, is the decode into the window: every access must drive the external port while it is in flight and must complete with a decode error (DECERR, the AMBA code for an address nobody decodes) carrying zero data. The zero-data requirement is DV-owned, the same rule `csr_read_decerr_zero` applies to every unmapped SMC hole: an error response carries no payload, so non-zero data means a live block answered in the window's place. An access that wedges or is answered by another block fails.
* The activity sampler keeps running for a few cycles after the access completes, so a request still being drained is not missed; a zero active-cycle count fails.
* The per-pad instance list and stride are checked against the chapter's 65 instances and 0x20 stride, and the last instance's address is checked to equal base plus index times stride, before the first, second and last instance are probed.

*Test Procedure*:

Drive `smc_external_window_pad_ctrl_decode_test_seq` on the SEP_IN AXI sequencer: probe the six control blocks, check the generated per-pad geometry against the chapter, probe per-pad instances 0, 1 and 64, require the access count to have reached its floor and print the cell ledger. The leaf then requires an external-port activity record for every probe with a non-zero active-cycle count, and books a protocol-VIP record.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-EXTWIN-PAD-CTRL` |The cell ledger: one line per control block and per-pad instance closed, naming the number of `clk_smc_i` cycles the access drove `smc_external_req_o` and the DECERR-with-zero-data answer it received. A cell is entered only after both of those held
|`CHK-EXTERNAL-WINDOW-PAD-CTRL-DECODE` |Summary line: the control-block and per-pad probe counts, the per-probe active-cycle counts, the measured per-pad stride and the instance count. Logged after every probe's routing and response compare and the stimulus floor have held
|`CHK-EXTWIN-PAD-CTRL-FLOOR` |The leaf requires one external-port activity record per expected probe and a non-zero active-cycle count on every one of them before this token is logged, so a run that stopped probing part-way fails
|===

*Pass Criteria*: all nine probes drive the adopter external AXI-Lite port for at least one cycle while in flight and complete DECERR with zero data, the generated per-pad geometry matches the chapter, and the leaf's per-probe activity check holds. Enrolled in: `all`, `hosted`.

'''''

[[sec-187-smc_gpio_pad_input_readback_test]]
===== 187 `smc_gpio_pad_input_readback_test`

*Source Module*: `smc_gpio_pad_input_readback_test`

*BFM / Model*: SEP_IN AXI driver for the GPIO CSRs; the bench external-pin path `tb_gpio_ext_drive_en` / `tb_gpio_ext_drive_value`; `tb_core2pad_en_o`, a mirror of the `core2pad_en_o` port

*Objective*: On six bonded GPIO wraps a pad input transition is captured in `DATA_CTRL.pad2core` in both directions, and TX enable raises and releases only that wrap's `core2pad_en_o` bit.

*Test Coverage*:

* The six wraps are chosen as bonded, input-by-default wraps that no LSIO function owns while the SPI mux is off and that no bench block drives permanently; the padring and `tb_top` bindings that rule the others out are recorded in the sequence.
* Each wrap's `core2pad_en_o` bit is required to read 0 before it is programmed, so the bit that later moves is the one this test caused to move; a wrap already enabled fails rather than being skipped.
* The pad is driven 0, then 1, then 0, and `DATA_CTRL.pad2core` is polled to follow each level within a bounded number of reads; the bound raises, and the number of reads each level needed is reported.
* While a wrap's output enable is programmed, exactly one bit of `tb_core2pad_en_o` may move: any other bit moving raises, which is what attributes the change to that wrap rather than to the write itself.
* `pad2core_en_o` has no vector probe in `tb_top` and `lsio_interface_select_o` is unconnected, so the RX direction enable and the interface-select output are not claimed.

*Test Procedure*:

Drive `smc_gpio_pad_input_readback_test_seq` on the SEP_IN AXI sequencer. Per wrap: require the enable bit clear, program the wrap as a register-controlled input, drive the pad 0/1/0 and poll `pad2core` to each level, release the bench drive, program the wrap as a register-controlled output and require its own enable bit to rise, restore `DATA_CTRL` and require the bit to clear. Afterwards require the access count and the scoreboard's SEP_IN item count to cover every access. The leaf requires every wrap to have completed both legs and books a protocol-VIP record.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-GPIO-PAD-INPUT-WRAP<wrap>` |Per wrap: the pad 0 -> 1 -> 0 sequence was followed in `DATA_CTRL.pad2core` inside the read bound at each level, and the wrap's own `core2pad_en_o` bit rose under TX enable and cleared on the restore without any other bit of the vector moving. One token per member of `WRAPS`, so the recorded name carries the wrap number
|`CHK-GPIO-PAD-INPUT-READBACK` |Summary line: the wraps that completed both legs and the SEP_IN access count, restating that `pad2core_en_o` and `lsio_interface_select_o` are not probed. Logged after every wrap's compares and the access floors have held
|`CHK-GPIO-PAD-INPUT-FLOOR` |The leaf requires every wrap in `WRAPS` to have completed both legs before this token is logged; the line prints the SEP_IN access count against the stimulus floor the sequence already asserted
|===

*Pass Criteria*: on every wrap, `DATA_CTRL.pad2core` follows the driven pad at all three levels inside the read bound, and the wrap's own `core2pad_en_o` bit rises and falls with the register while no other bit of the vector moves. Enrolled in: `all`, `hosted`.

'''''


[[sec-163-smc_gpio_irq_mask_deassert_test]]
===== 163 `smc_gpio_irq_mask_deassert_test`

*Source Module*: `smc_gpio_irq_mask_deassert_test`

*BFM / Model*: GPIO pad drive via `tb_gpio_ext_drive_*`; observation on `tb_gpio_irq_any`

*Objective*: Clearing DATA_CTRL.interrupt_enable must de-assert interrupt_o.

*Test Coverage*:

* The mask half of `hw/ip/gpio/doc/architecture.adoc:122`'s `interrupt_o = interrupt_enable && interrupt_trigger`, which no other GPIO test drives.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Program DATA_CTRL armed (RX enable, interface enable, interrupt enable, active-high) and prove the IRQ is low with the pad low.
. Drive the pad high, require the IRQ to assert, and hold it for 32 cycles.
. Clear only `interrupt_enable`, leaving the pad high and touching nothing else.
. Require the IRQ to fall and stay low.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-GPIO-IRQ-MASK-ARM` |Pad high with the enable set asserts the IRQ and holds it, so the mask leg's stimulus is the enable write alone
|`CHK-GPIO-IRQ-MASK-DEASSERT` |Clearing the enable de-asserts the IRQ and holds it de-asserted for the same span the arm leg holds it asserted
|===

*Pass Criteria*: `CHK-GPIO-IRQ-MASK-ARM` and `CHK-GPIO-IRQ-MASK-DEASSERT` hold after their compares. Enrolled in: `all`, `hosted`, `gpio`.

'''''

==== IRQ

[[sec-118-smc_irq_observe_test]]
===== 118 `smc_irq_observe_test`

*Source Module*: `smc_irq_observe_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip (AXI-Lite)

*Objective*: SMC OSS PyUVM interrupt-observe test.

*Test Coverage*:

* Sequence for smc_irq_observe_test.
* One IRQ SAMPLE of the three `tb_top` interrupt aggregates, consumed into the only property this leaf can honestly claim: **every aggregate reads its idle 0 and every one of the three probes carries a same-run liveness credit**, so the scoreboard exact-compared all three legs instead of booking them OBSERVED-ONLY.
* Why the credit is part of the assertion and not a comment: an idle `== 0` is a pure negative check, and the scoreboard *silently* downgrades an uncredited leg to `OBSERVED-ONLY (NOT checked evidence)` rather than failing.
* Without the gate below, dropping `probe_positive_controls` from the test would turn this testcase into an all-observed-only run that still passes.
* The credits come from the passive ledger in `env/smc_probe_liveness.py`, which is fed by a DUT observation (the probe seen at 1), so requiring them is DUT-sensitive: a stuck-at-0 / undriven / mis-bound aggregate fails here.
* `item.resolvable` is **not** presented as this testcase's check: every retained run is Verilator (2-state), where `value.is_resolvable` cannot be False, so it has no FAIL-ON path in the evidence that exists.

*Test Procedure*:

Drive `smc_irq_observe_test_seq`. Each checker below is a named compare the DUT can fail; a token is emitted only after that compare holds.


*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-IRQ-OBSERVE-IDLE-CHECKED` |Named compare in `smc_irq_observe_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|===


*Pass Criteria*: `CHK-IRQ-OBSERVE-IDLE-CHECKED` hold after their compares. Enrolled in: `all`, `hosted`, `irq`.
'''''

[[sec-120-smc_i2c_error_fifo_depth_test]]
===== 120 `smc_i2c_error_fifo_depth_test`

*Source Module*: `smc_i2c_error_fifo_depth_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip (AXI)

*Objective*: PROXY -- run I2C CSR decode plus SCL/SDA pin override depth checks.

*Test Coverage*:

* DEFENDS: I2C0 host/target CSR decode, and LSIO SCL/SDA release and pull-low behaviour at the pads.
* DOES NOT DEFEND: I2C error conditions or FIFO depth. This testcase runs the same `smc_i2c_master_target_test_seq` body as testcase 117 -- `FIFO_STATUS` is never read, the only `FIFO_CTRL` accesses are `RXRST | FMTRST` resets, and `CONTROLLER_EVENTS` is only written W1C to clear and read to decorate failure messages, so no event bit is ever asserted.
* GAP: FIFO-depth and error-injection coverage needs its own body. `smc_i2c_fifo_full_test` covers the full-FIFO case and `smc_i2c_p0_nack_test` the NACK case; neither is this testcase.

*Test Procedure*:

Drive `smc_i2c_error_fifo_depth_test_seq`. Each checker below is a named compare the DUT can fail; a token is emitted only after that compare holds.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-I2C0-HOST-WRITE` |Named compare in `smc_i2c_master_target_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-I2C0-HOST-REPEATED-START` |Two DUT-host write frames joined by a repeated START: the EEPROM VIP frames exactly two STARTs against one STOP on `tb_i2c0_*`, in `smc_i2c_master_target_test_seq.py`; the run fails otherwise
|`CHK-I2C0-OVRD-PAD` |Named compare in `smc_i2c_master_target_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-I2C0-SMBUS-ARA` |Named compare in `smc_i2c_master_target_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-I2C0-SMBUS-PEC` |Named compare in `smc_i2c_master_target_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-I2C0-U4-2-SMBUS` |Named compare in `smc_i2c_master_target_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|===


*Pass Criteria*: `CHK-I2C0-HOST-WRITE`, `CHK-I2C0-OVRD-PAD`, `CHK-I2C0-SMBUS-ARA`, `CHK-I2C0-SMBUS-PEC`, `CHK-I2C0-U4-2-SMBUS` hold after their compares. Enrolled in: `all`, `hosted`.
'''''

[[sec-123-smc_i2c_multi_instance_test]]
===== 123 `smc_i2c_multi_instance_test`

*Source Module*: `smc_i2c_multi_instance_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip (AXI)

*Objective*: I2C_1/2 + I2C_CTRL reset sweep and per-instance TARGET_ID co-resident patterns.

*Test Coverage*:

* I2C multi-instance CSR precheck over the three controllers (I2C_0/1/2) and the top-level I2C_CTRL block the generated map declares.
* Reset content: the I2C_1 and I2C_2 INTR_STATE bases and the I2C_CTRL base read their generated reset word as scoreboard value compares.
* Per-instance decode: every controller's `TARGET_ID` (plain rw storage, reset 0) holds a distinct target-address pattern at the same time and reads back its own before being restored, so a stride that aliases two controllers or an undecoded window answering OKAY with zeros fails the readback. The reset reads alone cannot make that distinction, since their reset word is zero.

*Test Procedure*:

Drive `smc_i2c_multi_instance_test_seq`. Each checker below is a named compare the DUT can fail; a token is emitted only after that compare holds.


*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-I2C-INSTANCE-CORESIDENT` |`TARGET_ID` of I2C_0, I2C_1 and I2C_2 hold three distinct patterns written before any is read back, each reads back its own and then its reset after the restore write, every readback a scoreboard value compare; an aliased pair or an OKAY-zero hole fails the first readback of the pair
|`CHK-I2C-INSTANCE-RESET-DECODE` |The I2C_1 and I2C_2 INTR_STATE bases and the I2C_CTRL base each returned OKAY and matched the reset word 0x0 in a scoreboard value compare, with the scoreboard checking all 3 accesses and booking exactly 3 value compares; a non-OKAY response, a non-zero word or a compare that never ran fails it
|===


*Pass Criteria*: `CHK-I2C-INSTANCE-CORESIDENT` and `CHK-I2C-INSTANCE-RESET-DECODE` hold after their compares and every sequence assert holds, including the exact access and value-compare counts. Enrolled in: `all`, `hosted`.

'''''

[[sec-132-smc_i2c_p0_timeout_test]]
===== 132 `smc_i2c_p0_timeout_test`

*Source Module*: `smc_i2c_p0_timeout_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip (AXI)

*Objective*: I2C1 stretch timeout measured against an I2C0 target proven live on the same shared bus.

*Test Coverage*:

* Allow leg first: the I2C0 target's TX FIFO is preloaded and the I2C1 host reads the byte back through RDATA while I2C0's ACQ FIFO shows the START word for the read address, so the two controllers are demonstrably on one bus before anything is timed.
* Timeout leg: the same read with the I2C0 TX FIFO empty. I2C1 raises `INTR_STATE.STRETCH_TIMEOUT` after the programmed count while I2C0 reports `INTR_STATE.TX_STRETCH` and holds the START word for that address, so the timeout is the host's measurement of the target's stretch and not of a stuck-low SCL with no target in the loop.
* Release leg: a byte written to I2C0 TXDATA ends the stretch; the host leaves its busy state and reads that byte back.
* Requires +smc_i2c_shared_bus.

*Test Procedure*:

Drive `smc_i2c_p0_timeout_test_seq`. Each checker below is a named compare the DUT can fail; a token is emitted only after that compare holds.


*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-I2C-P0-TIMEOUT` |I2C1 `INTR_STATE.STRETCH_TIMEOUT` set within the bounded poll while I2C0 `INTR_STATE.TX_STRETCH` is set and I2C0's ACQ FIFO holds the START word carrying the read address (`smc_i2c_p0_timeout_test_seq.py`); any of the three missing fails the run
|`CHK-I2C-P0-TIMEOUT-RELEASE` |After the timeout, a byte written to I2C0 TXDATA lets the I2C1 host leave its busy state and read exactly that byte (`smc_i2c_p0_timeout_test_seq.py`)
|`CHK-I2C-P0-TIMEOUT-TARGET-LIVE` |Positive control run first: with the I2C0 TX FIFO preloaded, the I2C1 host reads exactly that byte and I2C0's ACQ FIFO holds the START word for the read address (`smc_i2c_p0_timeout_test_seq.py`)
|`CHK-I2C-TIMEOUT-CSR-SWEEP` |Named compare in `smc_i2c_p0_timeout_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|===


*Pass Criteria*: `CHK-I2C-P0-TIMEOUT`, `CHK-I2C-P0-TIMEOUT-RELEASE`, `CHK-I2C-P0-TIMEOUT-TARGET-LIVE`, `CHK-I2C-TIMEOUT-CSR-SWEEP` hold after their compares. Enrolled in: `all`, `hosted`, `i2c`.
'''''

[[sec-134-smc_i2c_target_smbus_test]]
===== 134 `smc_i2c_target_smbus_test`

*Source Module*: `smc_i2c_target_smbus_test`

*BFM / Model*: I2C protocol VIP, ocah_axi_vip (AXI)

*Objective*: VIP ARA + external SMBSUS# into DUT I2C0 SMBus target.

*Test Coverage*:

* I2C0 target SMBALERT# + VIP ARA; external SMBSUS# on pad40.
* Not DUT-internal host SMBSUS.

*Test Procedure*:

|`CHK-I2C-TGT-SMBUS-ARA` |Named compare in `smc_i2c_target_smbus_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches

[[sec-135-smc_smbus_alert_ara_test]]
===== 135 `smc_smbus_alert_ara_test`

*Source Module*: `smc_smbus_alert_ara_test`

*BFM / Model*: I2C protocol VIP, ocah_axi_vip (AXI)

*Objective*: U4-2: DUT SMBALERT# pad39 + VIP ARA @0x0C (TB CSR stands in for FW).

*Test Coverage*:

* U4-2 SMBALERT# path: DUT I2C0 target asserts alert; VIP host runs ARA.
* TB-driven (no FW binary): AXI programs DUT as SMBus target @ ADDRESS0 with ADDRESS1=ARA(0x0C), preloads TXDATA with (addr<<1), sets SMBUS_CTRL.SMBALERT, observes pad39 low, then VIP master reads ARA and expects the alerting address byte.
* DUT hwclr clears SMBALERT# after address match.
* Honest scope: cocotb CSR stimulus stands in for FW alert assertion / ARA servicing; not a Rocket FW interrupt handler proof.

*Test Procedure*:

Drive `smc_smbus_alert_ara_test_seq`. Each checker below is a named compare the DUT can fail; a token is emitted only after that compare holds.


*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-SMBUS-ALERT-ARA` |SMBALERT# (pad39) idles high, goes low after SMBUS_CTRL.SMBALERT, the VIP host's ARA read at 0x0C returns the alerting address byte 0x20 (0x10 << 1), and hwclr returns the pad high after the address match. Fails on a pad that idles low, never asserts within the poll bound, an ARA reply other than 0x20, or a pad still low after the ARA
|===


*Pass Criteria*: `CHK-SMBUS-ALERT-ARA` holds after its compares and every sequence assert holds. Enrolled in: `all`, `hosted`.

'''''

* PMBus Linear11 encode/decode — pure math self-check (no bus traffic).

|Checker |Description

|`CHK-SMBUS-PEC-REF` |The VIP's SMBus PEC for the frame `[0xA0, 0x10, 0xA5]` equals the CRC-8/0x07 reference 0x6D computed independently, so the bus compare that follows does not grade its own arithmetic (`smc_smbus_pmbus_test_seq.py`)
|===


*Pass Criteria*: `CHK-SMBUS-ARA`, `CHK-SMBUS-PEC-REF` hold after their compares and every sequence assert holds (the PEC byte landing in the slave's memory, the Linear11/Linear16 round trips). Enrolled in: `all`, `hosted`.

'''''

[[sec-165-smc_i2c_intr_mask_test]]
===== 165 `smc_i2c_intr_mask_test`

*Source Module*: `smc_i2c_intr_mask_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip (AXI); observation on `tb_i2c_irq[0]`

*Objective*: The same interrupt-enable placement, on I2C: INTR_ENABLE must mask the output, not the set path.

*Test Coverage*:

* `CMD_COMPLETE`, whose `(event || test) && enable` set path at `i2c_core.sv:1001` is the exact shape under test.
* Both the held and the dropped consequence, as for the log engine.
* Every one of the twenty sources in the `irq_o` reduction, each raised as the only enabled source so the output move belongs to that term and no other. `i2c.rdl` splits them: a latched source is `woclr` in `INTR_STATE` with a `singlepulse` `INTR_TEST` field and is released by a W1C; a level source is read-only in `INTR_STATE` with a plain `rw` `INTR_TEST` field and is released by writing that field back to 0. A source the bench holds asserted cannot be swept from a measured clear, so it is named in the evidence and skipped, and the leg states the minimum number of sources it still requires.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Ungate the I2C peripheral clock and require instance 0's PLIC bit to read 0.
. Set INTR_ENABLE.CMD_COMPLETE, pulse INTR_TEST, require INTR_STATE to latch and the IRQ to assert, held 32 cycles.
. Clear INTR_ENABLE only and sample the IRQ.
. W1C the status, prove it cleared, then pulse INTR_TEST with INTR_ENABLE=0 and set the enable afterwards.
. Require INTR_STATE to hold the event.
. Sweep every source: clear it, enable it alone, raise it through INTR_TEST, require the IRQ to rise, release it and require the IRQ to fall with INTR_STATE clear.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-I2C-INTR-MASK-ARM` |The set path, the status register and the probe are all alive
|`CHK-I2C-INTR-MASK-DEASSERT` |Clearing the enable de-asserts the IRQ, holds it de-asserted for the same span the arm leg holds it asserted, and leaves INTR_STATE still carrying the bit -- the last is what separates an output mask from a DUT that simply clears the state
|`CHK-I2C-INTR-MASK-EVERY-SOURCE` |At least 16 of the twenty interrupt sources each raised the IRQ while it was the only enabled source, and released it again with `INTR_STATE` clear
|`CHK-I2C-INTR-MASK-LATCH` |An event arriving while masked is still latched and surfaces once the enable is set
|===

*Pass Criteria*: `CHK-I2C-INTR-MASK-ARM`, `CHK-I2C-INTR-MASK-DEASSERT`, `CHK-I2C-INTR-MASK-EVERY-SOURCE` and `CHK-I2C-INTR-MASK-LATCH` hold after their compares; the last three are measured before any raises, so none hides another. Enrolled in: `all`, `hosted`, `i2c`.

'''''

'''''

==== MAILBOX

[[sec-140-smc_mailbox_data_error_test]]
===== 140 `smc_mailbox_data_error_test`

*Source Module*: `smc_mailbox_data_error_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip (AXI)

*Objective*: Run mailbox write/read data and illegal access response checks.

*Test Coverage*:

* Mailbox data path and error-response depth test over real SEP_IN AXI.
* Every register address and the clock-gate field mask are imported by generated symbol (`hw/sys/smc/regs/gen/c/smc_addr.h` / `blocks/smc_base_config.h`), the way the sibling `smc_mailbox_irq_test_seq` does, instead of being reached as `MAILBOX_0_BASE_ADDR + 0x8/0x10/0x18` with a `1 << 1` field literal.
* The `CLOCK_GATE_CONTROL` offset is taken from the generated map rather than tracked in a comment, so a regenerated map moves this sequence with it ([ADDRESS-FROM-AUTHORITATIVE-MAP]).
* The DUT-vs-golden compare is the `expected=` on each paired `READ_DATA` read, enforced by `SmcScoreboard` (`env/smc_scoreboard.py:734-738`): data written into `OUTBOUND_WRITE_DATA` must come back out of `INBOUND_READ_DATA` in FIFO order, and vice versa.
* There are no synthetic `SmcMemoryModel` regions here: asserting a written literal back out of a TB-local dict at an address no DUT transaction reaches is a `C == C` compare that cannot fail on any RTL ([NO-ALWAYS-PASS-CHECKER]).
* The scoreboard's real `update_golden` / `check_golden` path cannot replace them either: it is keyed by `item.addr`, and a mailbox is a FIFO whose two writes land on the same address, so an address-keyed model cannot represent it.

*Test Procedure*:

Drive `smc_mailbox_data_error_test_seq`. Each checker below is a named compare the DUT can fail; a token is emitted only after that compare holds.


*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-MAILBOX-FIFO-DATA` |Named compare in `smc_mailbox_data_error_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-MAILBOX-IRQ-ASSERT` |Named compare in `smc_mailbox_vip_utils.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-MAILBOX-IRQ-CLEAR` |Named compare in `smc_mailbox_vip_utils.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-MAILBOX-IRQ-IDLE` |Named compare in `smc_mailbox_vip_utils.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-MAILBOX-IRQ-SOURCE` |Named compare in `smc_mailbox_vip_utils.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|===


*Pass Criteria*: `CHK-MAILBOX-FIFO-DATA`, `CHK-MAILBOX-IRQ-ASSERT`, `CHK-MAILBOX-IRQ-CLEAR`, `CHK-MAILBOX-IRQ-IDLE`, `CHK-MAILBOX-IRQ-SOURCE` hold after their compares. Enrolled in: `all`, `hosted`.
'''''

[[sec-142-smc_mailbox_field_sweep_test]]
===== 142 `smc_mailbox_field_sweep_test`

*Source Module*: `smc_mailbox_field_sweep_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip (AXI)

*Objective*: Every readable field of mailboxes 0-3 decodes, on the inbound port as well as the outbound one.

*Test Coverage*:

* The seven readable registers of each port, checked against `axil_mailbox.rdl:214-224` rather than trusted as a list: STATUS +0x010, ERROR_FLAGS +0x018, WIRQT +0x020, RIRQT +0x028, IRQS +0x030 (write-1-to-clear), IRQEN +0x038, IRQP +0x040 (`irqs_q & irqen_q`, read-only derived).
* Three of the ten registers per port are out, each for a stated reason. WRITE_DATA +0x000 and READ_DATA +0x008 are omitted because a read pops the paired port's FIFO, which is state this sweep must not disturb. CTRL +0x048 is `sw = w` flush strobes with nothing to read; its effect is covered by `smc_mailbox_flush_test`.
* Both ports, and the swept set **derived from the generated map** rather than listed: `_swept_fields` enumerates every `SMC_MAILBOX_<port>_MAILBOX_0_*_REG_OFFSET` symbol in `regs/gen/py/smc_reg.py` and removes the three that cannot be read. A hand-maintained list cannot guard itself -- an access count computed from the list the loop walks matches for any list content -- so deriving it from the map is what makes the anti-shrink claim true rather than circular.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Read CLOCK_GATE_CONTROL, set MAILBOX_CG_EN, and restore the original value at the end.
. Read all seven fields of mailboxes 0-3 on the outbound port, then the same on the inbound port.
. Require the access count to equal the number of readable registers the generated map holds, times the mailboxes, times the ports, plus the three clock-gate accesses.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-MBOX-FIELD-DECODE` |Every one of the 56 field reads returns OKAY. `csr_read` routes through the scoreboard's `item.resp_ok` assert, so a mis-decoded field answers DECERR or hangs and fails. `csr_read_bounded` is not used: it would tolerate a dead field and make the sweep vacuous
|`CHK-MBOX-FIELD-COUNT` |The access count matches the register count in the generated map, not the length of any list this testcase owns. Dropping a read from the loop fails; so does the map growing a register the sweep does not read. The one remaining way to narrow the sweep is to add a name to the `_UNREADABLE` table, which is pinned at three entries and requires a written reason, so it cannot happen silently
|===

*Pass Criteria*: both checkers hold and the scoreboard reports zero errors. Enrolled in: `all`, `hosted`.

'''''

[[sec-143-smc_mailbox_flush_test]]
===== 143 `smc_mailbox_flush_test`

*Source Module*: `smc_mailbox_flush_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip (AXI)

*Objective*: CTRL.wflush empties the write FIFO it claims to flush.

*Test Coverage*:

* `OUTBOUND_MAILBOX_0.CTRL` @0x48, which no other testcase reaches. `smc_mailbox_field_sweep_test` covers every other readable register of the port, on both directions, but cannot cover this one: a sweep reads, and CTRL is write-only.
* The register cannot be a readback test. `axil_mailbox.rdl` marks both fields as external, user-defined write effects: they are flush strobes, not storage, so a write/read-back pair would compare against whatever the register file returns for a write-only address and prove nothing. What is testable is the effect, and `STATUS.write_level_above_thresh` @0x10 bit 2 reports it.
* Addresses from `smc_addr.h`, field masks from `axil_mailbox.h`, rather than offsets added to a base ([ADDRESS-FROM-AUTHORITATIVE-MAP]).

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Enable the mailbox clock gate, then write WIRQT := 0 and read it back, so "above threshold" has a known meaning and the port is proven writable.
. Require `STATUS.write_level_above_thresh` to read 0 with nothing pushed.
. Push one word into WRITE_DATA and require the same bit to read 1.
. Write CTRL.wflush and require the bit to return to 0.
. Restore the clock-gate register.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-MBOX-FLUSH-ARM` |One word pushed with WIRQT=0 raises `STATUS.write_level_above_thresh`. This is the positive control: without it the check below is satisfied by a STATUS that reads zero for every reason including a dead one ([NEGATIVE-NEEDS-POSITIVE-CONTROL])
|`CHK-MBOX-FLUSH` |After CTRL.wflush the bit is clear again. Nothing in this bench drains the outbound write FIFO on its own -- its far side is the paired inbound port's read side, reached only by an explicit INBOUND_READ_DATA access -- so a level that falls between the two checks fell because of the flush
|===

*Pass Criteria*: both checkers hold: the level bit reads 0, then 0x4 after the push, then 0 after the flush. Enrolled in: `all`, `hosted`.

'''''

[[sec-147-smc_mailbox_multi_instance_test]]
===== 147 `smc_mailbox_multi_instance_test`

*Source Module*: `smc_mailbox_multi_instance_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip (AXI), ocah_axi_vip (AXI-Lite)

*Objective*: Mailbox STATUS sweep over every outbound and inbound instance the generated map exports (32 + 32, the `NUM_MAILBOXES` value `periphs.adoc` gives the SMC integration), IRQEN storage on instance 0 of each direction, and the last inbound instance raising its own bit of `mailbox_interrupts`.

*Test Coverage*:

* The instance count is derived from the `SMC_TOP_SMC_MAILBOX_{OUTBOUND,INBOUND}_MAILBOX_<n>_BASE_ADDR` symbols in `smc_addr.h` and required to equal the `periphs.adoc` value for both directions; a map that gained or lost an instance fails at import.
* Every instance's STATUS is read against the RDL idle word (EMPTY set, nothing else), so a mis-decoded or dead instance fails a scoreboard value compare rather than an OKAY-only read.
* IRQEN is the one register in the block the RDL declares as plain software-owned storage (`sw = rw; hw = r` with no acknowledge semantics), so it is the write/read-back leg; the RDL attributes of the other registers are the reason they are not swept here.
* The last inbound instance is armed for the write-threshold interrupt and one word is pushed; `mailbox_interrupts` must go from 0 to exactly that instance's bit and back to 0 when IRQEN is dropped. The STATUS word expected after the push is built from the RDL field descriptions and the `periphs.adoc` depth (2); the depth the DUT publishes in `CPU_CTRL.SMC_ATTRIBUTES` is compared against that value rather than used as its source, and the threshold rule (a write-threshold IRQ once the usage pointer exceeds WIRQT, which resets to 0) comes from the WIRQT description in `axil_mailbox.rdl`.

*Test Procedure*:

Drive `smc_mailbox_multi_instance_test_seq`. Each checker below is a named compare the DUT can fail; a token is emitted only after that compare holds.


*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-MAILBOX-CHANNEL-VECTOR` |The whole 32-bit `mailbox_interrupts` vector observed 0 before the push and with IRQEN armed, exactly the last instance's bit within a bounded poll after the push, and 0 again after IRQEN is dropped; every STATUS and IRQEN compare is a scoreboard value compare
|===


*Pass Criteria*: `CHK-MAILBOX-CHANNEL-VECTOR` holds after its compares and every sequence assert holds. Enrolled in: `all`, `hosted`.
'''''

==== UART

[[sec-149-smc_uart_baud_word_parity_format_test]]
===== 149 `smc_uart_baud_word_parity_format_test`

*Source Module*: `smc_uart_baud_word_parity_format_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip (AXI)

*Objective*: UART0 divisor x frame-format sweep, every axis observed where it is visible: frame timing and content on the TX pad, a driven frame on the RX pad, and the parity and framing detectors shown able to assert.

*Test Coverage*:

* Divisors 1 and 8 x the 16 WLS/STB/PEN/EPS combinations (stick parity excluded), 32 in all, with `MCR.LOOP` clear so the frames leave on pad 12 and enter on pad 11. Under `MCR.LOOP` the transmitter and receiver share one setting and every combination deframes the same byte, so the divisor, stop-bit and parity axes would be unfalsifiable there.
* TX pad: three queued bytes are decoded at simulation-time resolution on `tb_uart0_tx_from_dut` -- start bit, every data bit, parity bit and stop bit sampled at their bit centres -- and the shortest interval between consecutive start bits is compared with `(1 + data + parity + stop) x 16 x (DLL + 1)` `clk_periph_i` periods, with the stop count 1, 2 or 1.5 per `LCR.STB` and the word length and parity polarity per `LCR.WLS`/`LCR.PEN`/`LCR.EPS` (`uart_16550_main.rdl`). Each axis moves that measurement, so a DUT ignoring `DLL`, `STB`, `PEN` or `EPS` fails.
* RX pad: one frame in the same format is driven on `tb_uart0_rx_ext_drive` and read back through `RBR` with `LSR` clear of OE/PE/FE/BI.
* Error detectors: at each divisor a frame with the wrong parity bit raises `LSR.PE` and a frame with a low stop bit raises `LSR.FE`, both cleared again after the character is popped, so the no-error compares rest on detectors shown to fire in the same run.

*Test Procedure*:

Drive `smc_uart_baud_word_parity_format_test_seq`: ungate the UART clock and enable the UART0 pad function, then for each divisor and format program `DLL`/`DLH`/`LCR`, reset both FIFOs, queue three bytes and decode them on the TX pad, drive one frame on the RX pad and read it back, and after the sixteen formats drive the parity-error and framing-error frames. Each checker below is a named compare the DUT can fail; a token is emitted only after that compare holds.


*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-UART-BAUD-BASIC` |All 32 combinations completed, each with its frame length measured, and both error controls asserted (`smc_uart_baud_word_parity_format_test_seq.py`)
|`CHK-UART-BAUD-FMT` |One per combination: the three TX pad frames decoded to the queued bytes masked to the word length with the parity bit matching `EPS`, the shortest start-to-start interval equals one frame of the programmed format within two baud ticks, no interval is shorter than a frame, and the RX pad frame read back exactly through `RBR` with no LSR error (`smc_uart_baud_word_parity_format_test_seq.py`)
|`CHK-UART-FMT-FE-ASSERTS` |At each divisor a frame with a low stop bit sets `LSR.FE` with `DR`, and `FE` is clear after the pop (`smc_uart_baud_word_parity_format_test_seq.py`)
|`CHK-UART-FMT-PE-ASSERTS` |At each divisor an 8E1 frame with the parity bit inverted sets `LSR.PE` with `DR`, and `PE` is clear after the pop (`smc_uart_baud_word_parity_format_test_seq.py`)
|===


*Pass Criteria*: `CHK-UART-BAUD-BASIC`, `CHK-UART-BAUD-FMT`, `CHK-UART-FMT-FE-ASSERTS`, `CHK-UART-FMT-PE-ASSERTS` hold after their compares. Enrolled in: `all`, `hosted`, `uart`.
'''''

[[sec-151-smc_uart_extremes_misc_test]]
===== 151 `smc_uart_extremes_misc_test`

*Source Module*: `smc_uart_extremes_misc_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip (AXI)

*Objective*: UART0 SCR R/W + idle LSR.DR quiet check.

*Test Coverage*:

* UART0 SCR R/W without LCR/MCR/ECR side-effects; idle LSR has no DR.
* DEFENDS: only LSR.DR in the idle window, and only because this sequence has a same-run positive control (`_positive_dr_then_idle`) that drives DR to 1 and back to 0, so a stuck-at-0 DR fails.
* DOES NOT DEFEND: LSR.OE/PE/FE/BI. Nothing in this sequence can drive those bits to 1, so an idle-window check on them would be an unbacked negative that a tied-off bit passes identically. They are claimed by `smc_uart_error_conditions_test`, which raises PE and OE individually and break as FE and BI together, each and back; no testcase in this package raises FE on its own.

*Test Procedure*:

Drive `smc_uart_extremes_misc_test_seq`. Each checker below is a named compare the DUT can fail; a token is emitted only after that compare holds.


*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-UART-EXT-BASIC` |Named compare in `smc_uart_extremes_misc_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-UART-EXT-DR-POS` |Named compare in `smc_uart_extremes_misc_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches

|`CHK-UART-EXT-SCR` |Named compare in `smc_uart_extremes_misc_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|===


*Pass Criteria*: `CHK-UART-EXT-BASIC`, `CHK-UART-EXT-DR-POS`, `CHK-UART-EXT-IDLE`, `CHK-UART-EXT-SCR` hold after their compares. Enrolled in: `all`, `hosted`, `uart`.
