====== Shared sources with an external consumer

The build of the companion regression outside this repository resolves its
firmware includes against this tree's `fw/tests/` and `fw/include/`, so a
shared header has a consumer when that firmware includes it or when an
external-consumer image above includes it.

[cols="3,5",options="header"]
|===
|Source |Consumer
|`tests/cla_sanity_sequence.h`, `tests/uart_sanity_sequence.h`, `tests/reset_ctrl_sequence.h`
|Included by firmware of the companion regression outside this repository; `reset_ctrl_sequence.h` also by `reset_ctrl_sanity`. Closes with the last includer.
|`include/tt_smc_ecc.h`
|Included by `ecc_lint` and `ecc_pint`.
|`tests/coremark.h`, `tests/core_portme.h`, `tests/core_portme.c` and `FW_TEST_EXTRA_SRCS_coremark` in `fw.mk`
|The `coremark` image.
|`tests/cpu_perf.h`
|Included by `tests/core_portme.c` and the `coremark`, `matrix_mult`, `mergesort` and `quicksort` images. Closes with the last includer.
|===


===== Deferred images

Each row states the blocker and the consumer whose arrival closes it. The
`smc_fw_hello_world_test` pattern is a fire-and-forget CPU verdict on
`CPU_CTRL SCRATCH_0`; the `smc_fw_plic_claim_test` pattern adds an `arm_value`
the cocotb side waits for before it drives a stimulus. Rows that need a
rom-mode OCCP build share one trigger: the generic `[c_build.*]` is added with
the first image that consumes it, not ahead of it.

[cols="3,2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Image |Bucket |Blocker and what closes the row
|i2c_p1_back_to_back_xfer
|deferred
|The header promises eight back-to-back transfers but `main()` performs one 4-byte write (Step 4) and posts PASS; a wire decoder on the pad bus saw one START and one STOP. Closes when the image runs the transactions its header describes and is enrolled as `smc_fw_i2c_p1_back_to_back_xfer_test` on the `smc_fw_i2c_pair_test_seq` wire-floor pattern.
|mailbox_sanity
|deferred
|PLIC claim of a mailbox IRQ is provable only from the CPU; `smc_mailbox_irq_test` and `smc_mailbox_event_irq_test` observe the IRQ pin. Closes with a `smc_fw_plic_claim_test`-pattern leaf (`arm_value` plus an `on_armed` stimulus from cocotb) in `testlists/fw.toml` whose stimulus writes the mailbox after the 'interrupts set up' word.
|wdt_sanity
|deferred
|Cluster WDT bark, stuck handler, second-stage reset and revive need a running hart; `smc_wdt_key_csr_test` (`testlists/power.toml`) is CSR-only. Closes with a `smc_fw_plic_claim_test`-pattern leaf (`arm_value` plus an `on_armed` stimulus from cocotb) in `testlists/fw.toml` arming on scratch[7].
|xtrigger_gpio_override
|deferred
|GPIO hw2 override of the 15 xtrigger channels, sampled on the pads after scratch[5] arms; the cocotb alias `smc_xtrigger_gpio_override_test` is catalog-only in <<smc-deferred-disposition>>. Closes with a `smc_fw_plic_claim_test`-pattern leaf (`arm_value` plus an `on_armed` stimulus from cocotb) in `testlists/fw.toml`.
|rom_sanity
|deferred
|ROM window first/middle/last word decode; the cocotb alias `smc_dummy_rom_sanity_test` is catalog-only in <<smc-deferred-disposition>>. Closes with a `+rom_hex` asset carrying the three expected words and a `smc_fw_hello_world_test`-pattern leaf (`firmware = "rom_sanity"`, `+smc_scratch_ram_hex=rom_sanity.ecc.hex`, `+smc_hold_cpu_boot`) in `testlists/fw.toml`.
|rom_efuse_bits
|deferred
|ROM banks 1..3 decode and endian flip under eFuse scramble; the cocotb aliases `smc_rom_efuse_bits_0_test` / `_1_test` are catalog-only in <<smc-deferred-disposition>>. Closes with a scrambled four-bank `+rom_hex` asset, a matching `+smc_efuse_hex`, and a `smc_fw_hello_world_test`-pattern leaf (`firmware = "rom_efuse_bits"`, `+smc_scratch_ram_hex=rom_efuse_bits.ecc.hex`, `+smc_hold_cpu_boot`) in `testlists/fw.toml`.
|occp_comprehensive_error_verification_test
|deferred
|OCCP error codes and the SMC status ring against the status-coordination contract. Closes with a generic rom-mode `[c_build.*]` in `smc_sim_cfg.toml` that builds the production ROM plus `\{fw_target}`, added together with its first consumer, and a `smc_occp_sanity_test`-pattern leaf on the dual bench (`testlists/dual.toml`).
|occp_crc_err_injection_test
|deferred

Closes with a generic rom-mode `[c_build.*]` in `smc_sim_cfg.toml` that builds the production ROM plus `\{fw_target}`, added together with its first consumer, and a `smc_occp_sanity_test`-pattern leaf on the dual bench (`testlists/dual.toml`).

|SMC pad-arm for the xSPI GPIO override on the SMU external AXI path; SMU's `smu_sep_ext_axi_test` stages `smu_sep_ext_axi` instead. Closes by SMU-owner agreement: an SMU test that stages this image, or removal.
|i2c_p1_ackctrl
|deferred
|Programmable target ACK control; no cocotb body. Closes with a `smc_fw_hello_world_test`-pattern leaf (`firmware = "i2c_p1_ackctrl"`, `+smc_scratch_ram_hex=i2c_p1_ackctrl.ecc.hex`, `+smc_hold_cpu_boot`) in `testlists/fw.toml` following the tier-1 `i2c_sanity` leaf.
|i2c_p1_adrmask
|deferred
|Dual target address plus address mask; no cocotb body. Closes with a `smc_fw_hello_world_test`-pattern leaf (`firmware = "i2c_p1_adrmask"`, `+smc_scratch_ram_hex=i2c_p1_adrmask.ecc.hex`, `+smc_hold_cpu_boot`) in `testlists/fw.toml` following the tier-1 `i2c_sanity` leaf.
|i2c_p1_speedmode
|deferred
|Standard/Fast/Fast+ timing compliance; needs pad-timing observation from the TB. Closes with that observation and a `smc_fw_hello_world_test`-pattern leaf (`firmware = "i2c_p1_speedmode"`, `+smc_scratch_ram_hex=i2c_p1_speedmode.ecc.hex`, `+smc_hold_cpu_boot`) in `testlists/fw.toml`.
|i2c_p2_concurrent
|deferred
|Concurrent I2C instances; the shared SCL/SDA pad pair on this TB drives X under multi-master. Closes when each instance has its own pad pair.
|i2c_p2_mixmode
|deferred
|Controller and target in one instance; same shared-pad limitation. Closes when each instance has its own pad pair.
|cold_reset_lock_sanity
|deferred
|`RESET_UNIT.SS_COLD_RESET_LOCK`; the VPLAN card for `smc_reset_unit_lock_test` (`testlists/reset.toml`) cites this image as the firmware-side reference and the cocotb leaf proves the same two properties. Closes when the card cites the cocotb leaf alone, or with a `smc_fw_hello_world_test`-pattern leaf (`firmware = "cold_reset_lock_sanity"`, `+smc_scratch_ram_hex=cold_reset_lock_sanity.ecc.hex`, `+smc_hold_cpu_boot`) in `testlists/fw.toml`.
|cool_reset_isolate_cfg
|deferred
|`ISOLATE_REQ_*` configuration; runtime scored by `smc_cool_reset_from_pcie_test`, and firmware cannot observe its own cool reset. Cited by `smc_reset_unit_lock_test_seq`. Closes when the sequence cites the cocotb leaf alone, or with a `smc_fw_hello_world_test`-pattern leaf (`firmware = "cool_reset_isolate_cfg"`, `+smc_scratch_ram_hex=cool_reset_isolate_cfg.ecc.hex`, `+smc_hold_cpu_boot`) in `testlists/fw.toml`.
|avsbus_sanity
|deferred
|AVSBus controller command send; needs a pad-level AVSBus responder the bench lacks (the blocker of the waived `smc_sideband_avsbus_octs_bfm_test`). Closes with that VIP and a `smc_fw_hello_world_test`-pattern leaf (`firmware = "avsbus_sanity"`, `+smc_scratch_ram_hex=avsbus_sanity.ecc.hex`, `+smc_hold_cpu_boot`) in `testlists/fw.toml`.
|i2c_smbus_model_test
|deferred
|SMBus alert/suspend against an SV SMBus peer model this bench does not instantiate; `smc_smbus_alert_ara_test` and `smc_smbus_hostnotify_test` cover the VIP side. Closes with that model and a `smc_fw_hello_world_test`-pattern leaf (`firmware = "i2c_smbus_model_test"`, `+smc_scratch_ram_hex=i2c_smbus_model_test.ecc.hex`, `+smc_hold_cpu_boot`) in `testlists/fw.toml`.
|mailbox_int
|deferred
|Four-hart mailbox IRQ handlers, the per-hart variant of `mailbox_sanity`. Closes after the `mailbox_sanity` leaf lands, with a decision whether the per-hart split adds a checker.
|uart_log_engine_multi_entry_rr
|deferred
|Round-robin across triggered entries. Closes with a `smc_fw_hello_world_test`-pattern leaf (`firmware = "uart_log_engine_multi_entry_rr"`, `+smc_scratch_ram_hex=uart_log_engine_multi_entry_rr.ecc.hex`, `+smc_hold_cpu_boot`) in `testlists/fw.toml` after the tier-1 `uart_log_engine_single_entry` leaf proves the pattern.
|uart_log_engine_region_size
|deferred
|Per-entry slot address math. Closes with a `smc_fw_hello_world_test`-pattern leaf (`firmware = "uart_log_engine_region_size"`, `+smc_scratch_ram_hex=uart_log_engine_region_size.ecc.hex`, `+smc_hold_cpu_boot`) in `testlists/fw.toml` after the tier-1 `uart_log_engine_single_entry` leaf proves the pattern.
|uart_log_engine_intr
|deferred
|Log-engine `INTR_ENABLE` / `INTR_TEST` / `INTR_STATUS` truth table and W1C clear from the CPU. Its cocotb counterpart `smc_log_engine_intr_mask_test` (`testlists/uart.toml`, enrolled in `all`) proves from SEP_IN that `INTR_ENABLE` masks the output while `INTR_STATUS` latches and clears only on W1C, and passes; this image's expected table was written against the set-path gating RTL issue #1635 describes and is re-derived against that leaf's expectation before it is enrolled. Closes with a `smc_fw_hello_world_test`-pattern leaf `smc_fw_uart_log_engine_intr_test` (`firmware = "uart_log_engine_intr"`, `+smc_scratch_ram_hex=uart_log_engine_intr.ecc.hex`, `+smc_hold_cpu_boot`) in `testlists/fw.toml`, or by retiring this image in favour of the cocotb leaf that already proves the property.
|===
